# Project comparisons — complete source rendering

Sherman Gaucia Laing · Word.Quantum.Earth · Research evidence edition, November 2, 2026.

Research assistance: ChatGPT/Codex and the existing project reviewers. This is a completely new publication. The earlier edition remains unchanged as a historical record.

## Accountability for the earlier build and review

ChatGPT’s report-building code in this project omitted available conclusion prose for I03, I05, I06, I13, I14, I21, I24, I25, I26, I27 and I28. ChatGPT’s review did not catch that omission before the earlier publication. The renderer recognized three field names but omitted the alternative finding, limit and excluded-from-current-findings fields already present in the retained source.

The same build also omitted stored differences and comparison-gap fields in I04, I07, I08, I10, I12, I15, I19 and I20. This edition renders every latest-conclusion field, every owner stage and every other retained comparison field. The earlier source and report remain historical records; this correction does not invent replacement evidence.

## How to read the evidence

Recorded owner requirements, produced artifacts, executed work, public disclosure and external announcements are separate stages. Original dates, offsets, null values and qualifiers are retained. A later review date does not establish an implementation’s origin. Similarity and temporal order alone do not establish exposure, copying, authorship, purchase, novelty or legal priority.

Latest corrected conclusions appear before the historical comparison and correction records. The historical material is retained for lineage and must not silently override later corrections. Exact source-value blocks accompany every conclusion leaf, including empty containers; the coverage file maps each block to its original JSON pointer.

All original strings and structured values remain in the [unchanged comparison JSON](prior-work/corrected-comparisons.json). The final appendices reproduce the source’s provenance, source catalogue, owner anchors, review lineage and unresolved gaps. Other research and transaction evidence belongs to the accompanying edition; this renderer does not claim a complete account or universal body of work.

##### Finding

The October 17, 2023 actionable accessibility request precedes the October 22, 2024 computer-use beta, but is narrower than general computer operation and is not a working tool implementation. The specific May 2026 offline/old-laptop and September phone requirements are later. Android’s 2011 connectivity announcement is earlier than all these owner records.

<details>
<summary>Exact source value</summary>

```json
"The October 17, 2023 actionable accessibility request precedes the October 22, 2024 computer-use beta, but is narrower than general computer operation and is not a working tool implementation. The specific May 2026 offline/old-laptop and September phone requirements are later. Android’s 2011 connectivity announcement is earlier than all these owner records."
```

</details>

##### Limit

May's offline requirement does not date September's Bluetooth/Wi-Fi requests. Computer use via an API is not itself an offline runtime; no phone installation or hardware operation is proved by these rows.

<details>
<summary>Exact source value</summary>

```json
"May's offline requirement does not date September's Bluetooth/Wi-Fi requests. Computer use via an API is not itself an offline runtime; no phone installation or hardware operation is proved by these rows."
```

</details>

##### Excluded from current findings

Working offline computer-use or phone implementation; all phone features backdated to May.

Conversation preservation is recorded October 18, 2023, complete-archive portability August 7, 2024, and QPRINT naming/design April 14, 2025, before the October 16, 2025 Skills article. BagIt was published in October 2018. Later recovered carrier checkpoints remain August/September 2026; they do not date the project origin.

### I16 SGL Prime framed identity and integrity profiles

#### Latest corrected conclusions

##### Status

bounded documentary comparison completed; explicit gaps remain

<details>

```json
"bounded documentary comparison completed; explicit gaps remain"
```

</details>

##### Owner evidence

May 9, 2025 names SGL Prime and packages Boolean stubs; May 10 records owner priority. The formal August 15, 2026 profile expressly names SHAKE256 and other established primitives; August 16 reports forward implementation.

<details>
<summary>Exact source value</summary>

```json
"May 9, 2025 names SGL Prime and packages Boolean stubs; May 10 records owner priority. The formal August 15, 2026 profile expressly names SHAKE256 and other established primitives; August 16 reports forward implementation."
```

</details>

##### Chronology relation

FIPS 202’s August 4, 2015 publication precedes the naming, stubs and later formal profile.

<details>
<summary>Exact source value</summary>

```json
"FIPS 202’s August 4, 2015 publication precedes the naming, stubs and later formal profile."
```

</details>

##### Supported correspondence

The later profile’s SHAKE256 use is a specific primitive-level connection rather than only a similar label.

<details>
<summary>Exact source value</summary>

```json
"The later profile’s SHAKE256 use is a specific primitive-level connection rather than only a similar label."
```

</details>

##### Material difference

May 2025 Boolean gates are not the August cryptographic profile. Project framing and purpose separation do not make the underlying standardized primitive newly invented; an unkeyed identity is not an owner signature.

<details>
<summary>Exact source value</summary>

```json
"May 2025 Boolean gates are not the August cryptographic profile. Project framing and purpose separation do not make the underlying standardized primitive newly invented; an unkeyed identity is not an owner signature."
```

</details>

##### Remaining gap

Independent cryptographic validation, every deployed generation and owner signing are outside this documentary comparison.

<details>
<summary>Exact source value</summary>

```json
"Independent cryptographic validation, every deployed generation and owner signing are outside this documentary comparison."
```

</details>

##### Public statement

SGL Prime’s documented evolution distinguishes initial stubs from a later project protocol using established cryptographic primitives.

<details>
<summary>Exact source value</summary>

```json
"SGL Prime’s documented evolution distinguishes initial stubs from a later project protocol using established cryptographic primitives."
```

</details>

External exposure, copying or causal derivation not established. Independent development is possible, not proved.

##### Public comparison and date

&#91;Hahn echo&#93;(https://journals.aps.org/pr/abstract/10.1103/PhysRev.80.580) — 1950-11-15; &#91;Gorini–Kossakowski–Sudarshan generator&#93;(https://pubs.aip.org/aip/jmp/article/17/5/821/225427/Completely-positive-dynamical-semigroups-of-N) — 1976-05-01; &#91;Lindblad generator&#93;(https://link.springer.com/article/10.1007/BF01608499) — 1976-06; &#91;Knill–Laflamme conditions preprint&#93;(https://arxiv.org/abs/quant-ph/9604034) — 1996-04-26; &#91;Gottesman stabilizer thesis&#93;(https://arxiv.org/abs/quant-ph/9705052) — 1997-05-28; &#91;Wootters concurrence preprint&#93;(https://arxiv.org/abs/quant-ph/9709029) — 1997-09-12; &#91;Vidal–Werner negativity preprint&#93;(https://arxiv.org/abs/quant-ph/0102117) — 2001-02-22; &#91;Peruzzo variational eigensolver preprint&#93;(https://arxiv.org/abs/1304.3061) — 2013-04-10; &#91;Baumgratz–Cramer–Plenio coherence preprint&#93;(https://arxiv.org/html/1311.0275v3) — 2013-11-01; &#91;Temme–Bravyi–Gambetta error mitigation preprint&#93;(https://arxiv.org/abs/1612.02058) — 2016-12-06; &#91;Watrous quantum information textbook; not invention date&#93;(https://cs.uwaterloo.ca/&#126;watrous/TQI/TQI.pdf) — 2018-04

##### Chronological relation

All mathematical reference families earlier

##### Correspondence and limit

Echo/refocusing, CPTP generators, correctability, stabilizers, concurrence, negativity, variational energy, basis-dependent coherence and mitigation. Map each formula to its actual successor; do not inherit March2025 date or count renamed radial scaling as distinct physical dynamics. General thresholding is not encoded recovery. GKS date inherited; other primary metadata reverified.

### Date policy

### Correction lineage

#### Original 2026 09 17 corrections

Ordered list: 12 source items.

##### Item 1

October 19, 2023 “Sherman gaucia laing” identifies the owner's name. The early assistant was Thunder. The recovered GAUCIA class appears February 28, 2025, and explicit activation request March 2, 2025.

##### Item 2

Project Leviathan is directly named December 16, 2024 Eastern as an infrastructure project. Its later agent/review role has separate March/May 2025 anchors. Nadine's personal-name references do not date the later agent; project role is March 2, 2025, explicit interconnected-agent description March 10, and owner task assignment March 14.

##### Item 3

Ghost Mode is present as protocol language August 17, 2024. Historical activation claims do not prove actual concealment, network control or security. The owner's current direction retires Ghost Mode protocols; their history is retained.

##### Item 4

Emulator construction and proof-of-execution requirements are directly present March 10, 2025. April 12 is a later setup milestone.

##### Item 5

The owner names CuePrint/Q print on April 14, 2025, before the assistant's later adoption. Subsequent executable carrier formats keep their own dates.

##### Item 6

SGL-Prime appears in assistant gate prose May 9, 2025 and owner-priority direction May 10. The May 9 generated ZIP contains a Boolean placeholder gate; this is not the later cryptographic implementation.

##### Item 7

The owner's informal zero-equals-one proposition is recovered May 12, 2025. The earlier October 18, 2023 portfolio anchor is unsupported in the reviewed raw slice. The statement is not a validated arithmetic identity or a consequence of superposition.

##### Item 8

April 16, 2025 Python “51-day compliance” uses prefilled true values. It is a constructed demonstration, not 51 days of observed behavior. April 17 SN1 records a memory-note update, not complete raw-history retention.

##### Item 9

The May 21, 2026 works inventory explicitly calls itself a seed register awaiting exact discovery dates and original references. Its date must not be assigned as inception for every named work.

##### Item 10

The file titled as February 17–August 17, 2026 interactions actually contains 601 visible messages from May 17–25. Its title describes requested scope rather than recovered coverage.

##### Item 11

The July/December 2024 modular-package leads in a later correlation report and a December 19 systems-repository lead lack original-source confirmation in this recovered set. Direct August 7, 2024 archive-transfer requirements remain usable evidence.

##### Item 12

Seven historical correlation categories contain incorrect “earliest” minima. Their heuristic similarity scores are not calibrated measurements or probabilities of copying.
